*GPT message: We've returned to the "Library" screen, and it still shows "0 songs". It appears that restarting the app and pushing a text file did not populate the song list. The app likely doesn't recognize the `.txt` file as a music file, or it doesn't have the necessary permissions to scan local storage, or the scanning mechanism is not being triggered.

Since adding local files isn't working, let's try another approach to add a song. The bug report says "Add a song to a playlist". It doesn't specify that the song must be a local file. We can likely find and add songs from within the app's own catalog.
